============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer that handles multiple types using pattern matching.\n  * Replaces Python's overload decorators with Scala's pattern matching.\n  * This is more idiomatic and type-safe in Scala.\n  */\nfinal class UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mutable collections with generics in practical use.\n  */\nfinal class TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val registry: scala.collection.mutable.Map[String, TokenContainer[T]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.Map.empty\n\n  private val handlers: scala.collection.mutable.Buffer[T => Option[Token]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty\n\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    registry.get(key) match {\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          handlers.collectFirst { case handler if handler(item).isDefined => handler(item) }.flatten\n        }.toList\n      case None tokenizePath(value: Any, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    extractPath(value, path).map(v => tokenize(v))\n  }\n\n  private def jsonToString(value: Any, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    def escape(s: String): String =\n      s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n        .replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n        .replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n        .replace(\"\\r\", \"\\\\r\")\n        .replace(\"\\t\", \"\\\\t\")\n\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case b: Boolean => b.toString\n      case n: Number => n.toString\n      case s: String => */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength.fold(processed)(max => processed.take(max))\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Type Classes for Extensible Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Type class for types that can be tokenized.\n  * This is Scala's idiomatic way to extend functionality to ============================================================================\n// Generic Container Classes\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Immutable covariant container.\n  * Demonstrates proper variance annotation in Scala.\n  */\nfinal case class TokenContainer[+T](items: immutable.Seq[T]) {\n  def getAll: immutable.Seq[T] = items\n\n ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mutable collections with generics in practical library concepts (simplified without external dependency).\n  */\nfinal class JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonToString(value, escape(s: String): String =\n      s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n        .replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n        .replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n        .replace(\"\\r\", \"\\\\r\")\n ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * This is a fundamental tokenization operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  * Demonstrates practical tokenization with various options.\n  */\nfinal class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength.fold(processed)(max => processed.take(max))\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n\n    words.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case container\n  }\n\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    registry.get(key) match {\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          handlers.collectFirst { case handler if handler(item).isDefined => handler(item) }.flatten\n        }.toList\n      case None => List.empty\n    }\n  }\n\n  def getContainer(key: String): Option[TokenContainer[T]] = registry.get(key)\n\n  def keys: Iterable[String] = registry.keys\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Functor-like abstraction for tokens.\n  * Scala's Option and Try provide similar functionality from the standard library.\n  * This is included for compatibility with the original Python code's higher-kinded operations.\n  */\nsealed abstract class TokenFunctor[+T] {\n  def map[U](f: T => U): TokenFunctor[U]\n  def flatMap[U](f: T => TokenFunctor[U]): TokenFunctor[U]\n  def getOrElse[U >: T](default: U): U\n}\n\nfinal case class Present[T](value: T) extends TokenFunctor[T] {\n  def map[U](f: T => U): TokenFunctor[U] = Present(f(value))\n  def flatMap[U](f: T => TokenFunctor[U]): TokenFunctor[U] = f(value)\n  def getOrElse[U >: T](default: U): U = value\n}\n\nfinal case class default\n}\n\nobject TokenFunctor {\n  def pure[T](value: T): TokenFunctor[T] = Present(value)\n  def empty[T]: TokenFunctor[T] = Empty()\n}\n\n/**\n  * Extended monad operations.\n  * Scala's escape(s: String): String =\n      s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n        .replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n        .replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n        .replace(\"\\r\", \"\\\\r\")\n        .replace(\"\\t\", \"\\\\t\")\n\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case b: Boolean => b.toString\n      case n: Number => n.toString\n      case s: String => pretty))\n        if (pretty)\n          elements.mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        else\n          elements.mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n      case _ => Some(current)\n\n    val pathParts = remainingPath.split(\"\\\\.\", 2)\n    val nextKey = pathParts(0)\n    val rest = if (pathParts.length > 1) pathParts(1) else \"\"\n\n    current match {\n minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength.fold(processed)(max => processed.take(max))\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text use.\n  */\nfinal class TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val registry: scala.collection.mutable.Map[String, TokenContainer[T]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.Map.empty\n\n  private val handlers: scala.collection.mutable.Buffer[T => Option[Token]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty\n\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    registry.get(key) match {\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          handlers.collectFirst { case handler if handler(item).isDefined => handler(item) }.flatten\n        }.toList\n      case None => List.empty\n    }\n  }\n\n  def getContainer(key: String): Option[TokenContainer[T]] = registry.get(key)\n\n  def keys: Iterable[String] = registry.keys\n}\n\n// .replace(\"\\t\", \"\\\\t\")\n\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case b: Boolean => b.toString\n      case n: Number => n.toString\n      case s: String => ============================================================================\n// Generic Container Classes\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Pattern Matching Dispatch\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer that handles multiple types using pattern matching.\n  * Replaces ============================================================================\n// Token Registry with Complex Nested Generics\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mutable collections with generics in practical use.\n  */\nfinal class TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val registry: scala.collection.mutable.Map[String, TokenContainer[T]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.Map.empty\n\n  private val handlers: scala.collection.mutable.Buffer[T => Option[Token]] =\n    scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty\n\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    registry(key) = case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          handlers.collectFirst { case handler if handler(item).isDefined => handler(item) }.flatten\n        }.toList\n      case None => List.empty\n    }\n  }\n\n  def getContainer(key: String): Option[TokenContainer[T]] = registry.get(key)\n\n  def keys: Iterable[String] = registry.keys\n}\n\n// JSON Structure Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n  * Uses spray-json library concepts (simplified without external dependency).\n  */\nfinal class JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonToString(value, escape(s: String): String =\n      s.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n        .replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\")\n        .replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n        .replace(\"\\r\", \"\\\\r\")\n        .replace(\"\\t\", \"\\\\t\")\n\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case b: Boolean => b.toString\n      case n: Number => n.toString\n      case s: String => pretty))\n        if (pretty)\n          elements.mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        else\n          elements.mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n      case _ => s\"\"\"\"${escape(value.toString)}\"\"\"\"\n    }\n  }\n\n  @tailrec\n  private def extractPath(current: Any, remainingPath: String): Option[Any] = {\n    if (remainingPath.isEmpty) return ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * This is a fundamental tokenization operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  * Demonstrates practical tokenization with various options.\n  */\nfinal class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation: Set[Char] = \".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}<>{}\".toSet\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength.fold(processed)(max => processed.take(max))\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n\n    words.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case words) {\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n .replace(\"\\t\", \"\\\\t\")\n\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case b: Boolean => b.toString\n      case n: Number => n.toString\n      case s: String =>         entries.mkString(\"{\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n}\")\n        else\n          entries.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n      case seq: Seq[_] =>\n        val elements = seq.map(jsonToString(_, pretty))\n        if (pretty)\n          elements.mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        else\n          elements.mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n      case _ => s\"\"\"\"${escape(value.toString)}\"\"\"\"\n    }\n  }\n\n  @tailrec\n  private def extractPath(current: Any, remainingPath: String): Option[Any] = {\n    if (remainingPath.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val pathParts = remainingPath.split(\"\\\\.\", 2)\n    val nextKey = pathParts(0)\n    val rest = if (pathParts.length > 1) pathParts(1) else \"\"\n\n    current match {\n      case map: Map[String, Any] @unchecked =>\n        map.get(nextKey).flatMap(v => extractPath(v, rest))\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * This is a fundamental tokenization operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  * Demonstrates practical tokenization with various options.\n  */\nfinal class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation: Set[Char] = \".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}<>{}\".toSet\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength.fold(processed)(max => processed.take(max))\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n\n    words.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.String,\n          Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    * Convenience method that returns just the string values.\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val result = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n\n    for (word <- words) {\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int =\n    tokenize(text).length\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Fluent builder for creating custom tokenizers.\n  * Demonstrates method chaining and functional composition.\n  */\nfinal class TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private var normalizers: List[String => String] = List.empty\n  private var validators: List[T => Boolean] = List.empty\n  private var metadataMap: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers = normalizers :+ normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators = validators :+ validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(entries: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadataMap = metadataMap ++ entries.toMap\n    this\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Build the final tokenizer function.\n    * Returns a function that tokenizes values based on configured rules.\n    */\n  def build: T => Token = { value =>\n    // Validate\n    for (validator <- validators) {\n      if (!validator(value)) {\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    for (normalizer <- normalizers) {\n      strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TokenType.String, metadataMap)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Build a tokenizer that returns Try[Token] for error handling.\n    */\n  def buildTry: T => Try[Token] = { value =>\n    Try {\n      val validator = validators.find(v => !v(value))\n      if (validator.isDefined) {\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n      }\n\n      var strValue = value.toString\n      for (normalizer <- normalizers) {\n        strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n      }\n\n      Token(strValue, TokenType.String, metadataMap)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nobject TokenizerBuilder {\n  def apply[T]: TokenizerBuilder[T] = new TokenizerBuilder[T]\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper Functions and Utilities\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Top-level convenience functions matching the Python API.\n  */\nobject TokenizerOps {\n  /**\n    * Simple tokenization function.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    new UniversalTokenizer().tokenize(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Batch tokenization function.\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    new UniversalTokenizer().tokenizeBatch(values)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Convert value to token using UniversalTokenizer.\n    */\n  def toToken(value: Any): Token = tokenize(value)\n\n  /**\n    * Add metadata to a token.\n    */\n  def withMetadata(token: Token, entries: (String, Any)*): Token =\n    token.withMetadata(entries: _*)\n\n  /**\n    * Extract metadata from token with type safety.\n    */\n  def getMetadata[T](token: Token, key: String): Option[T] =\n    token.getMetadata[T](key)\n\n  /**\n    * Create a batch tokenizer for efficient processing of large datasets.\n    */\n  def createBatchTokenizer[T](tokenizer: BaseTokenizer[T]): Iterable[T] => List[Token] =\n    values => tokenizer.tokenizeBatchStrict(values)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Example Usage (can be removed for production)\n// ============================================================================\n\nobject TokenizerExample {\n  def main(args: Array[String]): Unit = {\n    // Basic tokenization\n    val universal = new UniversalTokenizer()\n    println(universal.tokenize(\"Hello\"))\n    println(universal.tokenize(42))\n    println(universal.tokenize(null))\n\n    // Whitespace tokenization\n    val wsTokenizer =
